/* ── Safe storage ── */
const MemoryStore = new Map();
const SafeStore = {
get(key, fallback = null) {
try {
const v = localStorage.getItem(key);
if (v == null) return MemoryStore.has(key) ? MemoryStore.get(key) : fallback;
return JSON.parse(v);
} catch (_) {
return MemoryStore.has(key) ? MemoryStore.get(key) : fallback;
}
},
set(key, value) {
MemoryStore.set(key, value);
try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value)); return true; }
catch (_) { return false; }
},
remove(key) {
MemoryStore.delete(key);
try { localStorage.removeItem(key); } catch (_) {}
}
};

/* ── Localization ── */
const L10N = {
lang: 'bn',
bus: null,
init(bus) {
this.bus = bus;
const saved = SafeStore.get('nodiBachao_settings_v1');
if (saved && saved.language) this.lang = saved.language;
else this.lang = GameConfig.settings.language;
this.applyDom();
},
t(key) {
const pack = STRINGS[this.lang] || STRINGS.bn;
const v = pack[key];
if (v != null) return v;
return (STRINGS.en[key] != null ? STRINGS.en[key] : key);
},
setLang(lang) {
if (!STRINGS[lang]) return;
this.lang = lang;
document.documentElement.lang = lang === 'bn' ? 'bn' : (lang === 'ko' ? 'ko' : 'en');
this.applyDom();
if (this.bus) this.bus.emit('locale:changed', lang);
},
wasteName(type) {
return this.t('w_' + type) || type;
},
dateLocale() {
if (this.lang === 'bn') return 'bn-BD';
if (this.lang === 'ko') return 'ko-KR';
return 'en-GB';
},
applyDom() {
document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach((el) => {
const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
const val = this.t(key);
if (typeof val === 'string') el.textContent = val;
});
}
};

/* ── Event bus ── */
class EventBus {
constructor() { this._listeners = new Map(); }
on(event, handler) {
if (!this._listeners.has(event)) this._listeners.set(event, new Set());
this._listeners.get(event).add(handler);
return () => this.off(event, handler);
}
once(event, handler) {
const off = this.on(event, (p) => { off(); handler(p); });
return off;
}
off(event, handler) {
const set = this._listeners.get(event);
if (set) set.delete(handler);
}
emit(event, payload) {
const set = this._listeners.get(event);
if (!set) return;
for (const h of [...set]) {
try { h(payload); } catch (err) { console.error(`[EventBus] ${event}`, err); }
}
}
clear() { this._listeners.clear(); }
}

/* ── Game states ── */
const GameState = Object.freeze({
BOOT: 'BOOT', LOADING: 'LOADING', MAIN_MENU: 'MAIN_MENU', NAME: 'NAME',
HOWTO: 'HOWTO', SDG: 'SDG', SETTINGS: 'SETTINGS', SCOREBOARD: 'SCOREBOARD',
TUTORIAL: 'TUTORIAL', PLAYING: 'PLAYING', PAUSED: 'PAUSED',
COUNTDOWN: 'COUNTDOWN', WAVE_SUMMARY: 'WAVE_SUMMARY', RESULT: 'RESULT',
CONTEXT_LOST: 'CONTEXT_LOST', NO_WEBGL: 'NO_WEBGL',
});

class GameStateManager {
constructor(bus) {
this.bus = bus;
this.current = GameState.BOOT;
this.previous = null;
this._returnFrom = null;
this._transitions = {
[GameState.BOOT]: [GameState.LOADING, GameState.NO_WEBGL],
[GameState.LOADING]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.CONTEXT_LOST],
[GameState.MAIN_MENU]: [GameState.NAME, GameState.HOWTO, GameState.SDG, GameState.SETTINGS, GameState.SCOREBOARD],
[GameState.NAME]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.TUTORIAL, GameState.PLAYING],
[GameState.HOWTO]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU],
[GameState.SDG]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU],
[GameState.SETTINGS]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.PAUSED],
[GameState.SCOREBOARD]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.RESULT],
[GameState.TUTORIAL]: [GameState.PLAYING, GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.PAUSED],
[GameState.PLAYING]: [GameState.PAUSED, GameState.WAVE_SUMMARY, GameState.RESULT, GameState.COUNTDOWN],
[GameState.PAUSED]: [GameState.COUNTDOWN, GameState.SETTINGS, GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.PLAYING, GameState.TUTORIAL],
[GameState.COUNTDOWN]: [GameState.PLAYING, GameState.PAUSED],
[GameState.WAVE_SUMMARY]: [GameState.PLAYING, GameState.RESULT],
[GameState.RESULT]: [GameState.MAIN_MENU, GameState.SCOREBOARD, GameState.NAME, GameState.PLAYING, GameState.TUTORIAL],
[GameState.CONTEXT_LOST]: [],
[GameState.NO_WEBGL]: [],
};
}
canTransition(to) {
return (this._transitions[this.current] || []).includes(to);
}
setState(to, payload = {}) {
if (to === this.current) return false;
if (!this.canTransition(to)) {
console.warn(`[GameState] blocked: ${this.current} → ${to}`);
return false;
}
this.previous = this.current;
this.current = to;
this.bus.emit('state:changed', { from: this.previous, to, payload });
return true;
}
force(to, payload = {}) {
this.previous = this.current;
this.current = to;
this.bus.emit('state:changed', { from: this.previous, to, payload });
}
is(s) { return this.current === s; }
isPlayingLike() {
return this.current === GameState.PLAYING || this.current === GameState.TUTORIAL;
}
}

/* ── Settings ── */
class SettingsManager {
constructor(bus) {
this.bus = bus;
this.data = { ...GameConfig.settings };
const saved = SafeStore.get('nodiBachao_settings_v1');
if (saved && typeof saved === 'object') Object.assign(this.data, saved);
this.resolvedQuality = 'medium';
this._fpsLowFrames = 0;
}
save() { SafeStore.set('nodiBachao_settings_v1', this.data); }
get() { return this.data; }
set(key, value) {
this.data[key] = value;
this.save();
this.bus.emit('settings:changed', { key, value, data: this.data });
if (key === 'graphics') this.bus.emit('settings:graphics', this.getResolvedQuality());
if (key === 'language') L10N.setLang(value);
if (key === 'reducedMotion') this._applyMotion();
if (key === 'musicOn' || key === 'musicVol' || key === 'sfxOn' || key === 'sfxVol') {
this.bus.emit('settings:audio', this.data);
}
}
detectAutoQuality() {
const cores = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4;
const mem = navigator.deviceMemory || 4;
const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
const mobile = /Mobi|Android|iPhone|iPad/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
// Prefer stable FPS on phones and high-DPR screens
if (mobile) return (mem <= 4 || cores <= 6 || dpr >= 2) ? 'low' : 'medium';
if (dpr > 2 || mem <= 4 || cores <= 4) return 'medium';
if (mem >= 8 && cores >= 6) return 'high';
return 'medium';
}
getResolvedQuality() {
if (this.data.graphics === 'auto') return this.resolvedQuality || this.detectAutoQuality();
return this.data.graphics;
}
bootResolve() {
this.resolvedQuality = this.detectAutoQuality();
this._applyMotion();
L10N.setLang(this.data.language);
}
noteFps(fps) {
if (this.data.graphics !== 'auto') return;
if (fps > 0 && fps < 28) {
this._fpsLowFrames++;
if (this._fpsLowFrames > 8) {
this._fpsLowFrames = 0;
const order = ['high', 'medium', 'low'];
const i = order.indexOf(this.resolvedQuality);
if (i >= 0 && i < order.length - 1) {
this.resolvedQuality = order[i + 1];
this.bus.emit('settings:graphics', this.resolvedQuality);
}
}
} else {
this._fpsLowFrames = Math.max(0, this._fpsLowFrames - 1);
}
}
_applyMotion() {
const pref = this.data.reducedMotion;
const system = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
const on = pref === 'on' || (pref === 'auto' && system);
document.body.classList.toggle('reduced-motion', on);
this.reducedMotionActive = on;
}
wantsReducedMotion() { return !!this.reducedMotionActive; }
}

/* ── Leaderboard / profile ── */
class LeaderboardManager {
constructor() {
this.key = 'nodiBachao_level1_scores_v1';
this.profileKey = 'nodiBachao_profile_v1';
this.nameKey = 'nodiBachao_lastName_v1';
}
getScores() {
const list = SafeStore.get(this.key, []);
return Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
}
getLastName() {
const n = SafeStore.get(this.nameKey, '');
return typeof n === 'string' ? n : '';
}
setLastName(name) { SafeStore.set(this.nameKey, name); }
getProfile() {
return SafeStore.get(this.profileKey, {
totalScore: 0, gamesPlayed: 0, wasteCollected: 0, bestStars: { level1: 0 }
});
}
addResult(entry) {
const scores = this.getScores();
const prevBest = scores.length ? scores[0].score : 0;
scores.push(entry);
scores.sort((a, b) => {
if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
if (b.stars !== a.stars) return b.stars - a.stars;
if (a.pollution !== b.pollution) return a.pollution - b.pollution;
return b.at - a.at;
});
const top = scores.slice(0, 10);
SafeStore.set(this.key, top);
const profile = this.getProfile();
profile.totalScore = (profile.totalScore || 0) + entry.score;
profile.gamesPlayed = (profile.gamesPlayed || 0) + 1;
profile.wasteCollected = (profile.wasteCollected || 0) + (entry.collected || 0);
profile.bestStars = profile.bestStars || {};
profile.bestStars.level1 = Math.max(profile.bestStars.level1 || 0, entry.stars || 0);
SafeStore.set(this.profileKey, profile);
const inTop = top.some((r) => r.at === entry.at && r.name === entry.name && r.score === entry.score);
const isHigh = inTop && entry.score >= prevBest;
return { top, isHigh };
}
clear() { SafeStore.set(this.key, []); }
validateName(raw) {
const name = String(raw || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 16) return { ok: false, name };
return { ok: true, name };
}
}

/* ── Haptics ── */
class HapticsManager {
constructor(settings) { this.settings = settings; }
supported() { return typeof navigator.vibrate === 'function'; }
pulse(pattern) {
if (!this.settings.get().vibration || !this.supported()) return;
try { navigator.vibrate(pattern); } catch (_) {}
}
grab() { this.pulse(12); }
collect() { this.pulse(18); }
miss() { this.pulse([20, 40, 20]); }
wave() { this.pulse([30, 40, 50]); }
}

/* ── Audio ── */
class AudioManager {
constructor(bus, settings) {
this.bus = bus;
this.settings = settings;
this.ctx = null;
this.master = null;
this.musicGain = null;
this.sfxGain = null;
this.ambGain = null;
this.rainGain = null;
this.unlocked = false;
this._bedNodes = [];
this._timers = [];
this._muted = false;
this._rainLevel = 0;
this._musicStep = 0;
bus.on('settings:audio', () => this._applyGains());
bus.on('weather:thunder', () => this.play('thunder'));
bus.on('weather:flood', ({ on }) => { if (on) this.play('flood'); });
bus.on('audio:floodHorns', ({ on }) => this.setFloodHorns(on));
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
if (!this.ctx) return;
if (document.hidden) this.ctx.suspend().catch(() => {});
else if (this.unlocked) this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
});
}
async unlock() {
if (this.unlocked) return;
const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
if (!AC) return;
this.ctx = new AC();
this.master = this.ctx.createGain();
this.musicGain = this.ctx.createGain();
this.sfxGain = this.ctx.createGain();
this.ambGain = this.ctx.createGain();
this.rainGain = this.ctx.createGain();
this.rainGain.gain.value = 0.0001;
this.musicGain.connect(this.master);
this.sfxGain.connect(this.master);
this.ambGain.connect(this.master);
this.rainGain.connect(this.ambGain);
this.master.connect(this.ctx.destination);
this._buffers = {};
this._sampleLoops = {};
this._applyGains();
if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') await this.ctx.resume();
this.unlocked = true;
await this._loadSamples();
this._startAmbient();
this._startMusic();
const tap = document.getElementById('btn-load-audio');
if (tap) { tap.hidden = true; tap.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); }
}
async _loadSamples() {
const specs = {
rain: [
'assets/audio/rain.wav',
'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/anars/blank-audio@master/250-milliseconds-of-silence.mp3',
],
thunder: ['assets/audio/thunder.wav'],
music: ['assets/audio/music.wav'],
};
for (const [key, urls] of Object.entries(specs)) {
for (const url of urls) {
// silence CDN probe is not a usable bed — skip
if (url.includes('silence')) continue;
try {
const res = await fetch(url);
if (!res.ok) continue;
const arr = await res.arrayBuffer();
this._buffers[key] = await this.ctx.decodeAudioData(arr.slice(0));
break;
} catch (_) { /* try next / fall back to synth */ }
}
}
}
_playBuffer(name, dest, { loop = false, vol = 1, when = 0 } = {}) {
const buf = this._buffers && this._buffers[name];
if (!buf || !this.ctx || !dest) return null;
const src = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
const g = this.ctx.createGain();
src.buffer = buf;
src.loop = loop;
g.gain.value = vol;
src.connect(g); g.connect(dest);
const t = this.ctx.currentTime + when;
src.start(t);
this._bedNodes.push(src);
return { src, gain: g };
}
_applyGains() {
if (!this.ctx) return;
const s = this.settings.get();
const mute = this._muted ? 0 : 1;
this.musicGain.gain.value = (s.musicOn ? s.musicVol : 0) * mute * 0.9;
this.sfxGain.gain.value = (s.sfxOn ? s.sfxVol : 0) * mute;
this.ambGain.gain.value = (s.sfxOn ? Math.max(0.4, s.sfxVol) : 0) * mute * 0.75;
}
toggleMute() {
this._muted = !this._muted;
this._applyGains();
return this._muted;
}
isMuted() { return this._muted; }
setRainLevel(v) {
this._rainLevel = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(v, 0, 1);
if (!this.rainGain || !this.ctx) return;
const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
// Sample rain is louder; synth rain quieter
const mul = this._buffers && this._buffers.rain ? 0.55 : 0.085;
const target = 0.0001 + this._rainLevel * mul;
this.rainGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);
this.rainGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(target, t + 0.35);
}
setFloodHorns(on) { this._floodHorns = !!on; }
_tone(freq, dur, type, gainNode, vol = 0.08, when = 0) {
if (!this.ctx || !gainNode) return;
const t = this.ctx.currentTime + when;
const o = this.ctx.createOscillator();
const g = this.ctx.createGain();
o.type = type;
o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0002, vol), t + 0.02);
g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
o.connect(g); g.connect(gainNode);
o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
}
_noiseBurst(dur, vol, dest, lowpass = 1200, when = 0) {
if (!this.ctx || !dest) return;
const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(this.ctx.sampleRate * dur));
const buf = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, n, this.ctx.sampleRate);
const data = buf.getChannelData(0);
for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n);
const src = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
const g = this.ctx.createGain();
const f = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = lowpass;
const t = this.ctx.currentTime + when;
src.buffer = buf;
g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0002, vol), t + 0.01);
g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
src.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(dest);
src.start(t); src.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}
_loopNoise(dest, vol, lowpass, highpass = 0) {
if (!this.ctx) return null;
const len = this.ctx.sampleRate * 2;
const buf = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, len, this.ctx.sampleRate);
const data = buf.getChannelData(0);
for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
const src = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
src.buffer = buf;
src.loop = true;
const g = this.ctx.createGain();
g.gain.value = vol;
const lp = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = lowpass;
src.connect(lp);
if (highpass > 0) {
const hp = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
hp.type = 'highpass'; hp.frequency.value = highpass;
lp.connect(hp); hp.connect(g);
} else {
lp.connect(g);
}
g.connect(dest);
src.start();
this._bedNodes.push(src);
return { src, gain: g, filter: lp };
}
play(name) {
if (!this.unlocked || !this.ctx) return;
switch (name) {
case 'grab':
this._tone(240, 0.07, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.07);
this._noiseBurst(0.06, 0.03, this.sfxGain, 1800);
break;
case 'hover':
case 'lid':
this._tone(480, 0.08, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.045);
this._tone(720, 0.06, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.025, 0.04);
break;
case 'collect':
this._tone(523, 0.1, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.08);
this._tone(784, 0.14, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.06, 0.06);
this._tone(1046, 0.12, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.04, 0.12);
break;
case 'combo':
this._tone(587, 0.08, 'square', this.sfxGain, 0.04);
this._tone(880, 0.1, 'square', this.sfxGain, 0.035, 0.05);
this._tone(1174, 0.12, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.03, 0.1);
break;
case 'miss':
this._noiseBurst(0.3, 0.06, this.sfxGain, 700);
this._tone(98, 0.28, 'sawtooth', this.sfxGain, 0.045);
break;
case 'throw':
this._noiseBurst(0.12, 0.05, this.sfxGain, 1400);
this._tone(180, 0.1, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.04);
break;
case 'jam':
this._noiseBurst(0.35, 0.07, this.sfxGain, 500);
this._tone(90, 0.25, 'sawtooth', this.sfxGain, 0.05);
this._tone(140, 0.2, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.03, 0.08);
break;
case 'flood':
this._noiseBurst(0.8, 0.09, this.sfxGain, 400);
this._tone(70, 0.6, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.05);
this._tone(110, 0.5, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.035, 0.15);
break;
case 'thunder':
if (this._buffers && this._buffers.thunder) {
this._playBuffer('thunder', this.sfxGain, { vol: 0.85 });
} else {
this._noiseBurst(0.15, 0.17, this.sfxGain, 2600);
this._noiseBurst(1.7, 0.15, this.sfxGain, 260, 0.09);
this._noiseBurst(1.4, 0.11, this.sfxGain, 150, 0.42);
this._noiseBurst(1.1, 0.07, this.sfxGain, 90, 0.95);
}
break;
case 'wave':
this._tone(392, 0.16, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.065);
this._tone(523, 0.2, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.05, 0.12);
break;
case 'perfect':
this._tone(659, 0.18, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.065);
this._tone(880, 0.22, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.05, 0.1);
this._tone(1318, 0.25, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.04, 0.2);
break;
case 'win':
[523, 659, 784, 1046].forEach((f, i) => this._tone(f, 0.24, 'sine', this.sfxGain, 0.055, i * 0.12));
break;
case 'lose':
this._tone(180, 0.45, 'sawtooth', this.sfxGain, 0.055);
this._tone(120, 0.55, 'triangle', this.sfxGain, 0.045, 0.15);
this._noiseBurst(0.5, 0.05, this.sfxGain, 500, 0.1);
break;
default: break;
}
}
_startAmbient() {
if (!this.ctx) return;
// Prefer real rain loop; keep synth beds for wind/traffic/birds
if (this._buffers.rain) {
this._playBuffer('rain', this.rainGain, { loop: true, vol: 1 });
} else {
this._loopNoise(this.rainGain, 1.0, 2200, 400);
}
this._loopNoise(this.ambGain, 0.028, 320); // waves
this._loopNoise(this.ambGain, 0.012, 900, 200); // wind
this._loopNoise(this.ambGain, 0.009, 700, 150); // crowd
this._loopNoise(this.ambGain, 0.014, 180); // traffic
// Soft rain even on menu so loader feels alive after tap
this.setRainLevel(0.22);
const bird = () => {
if (!this.unlocked || !this.ctx) return;
if (Math.random() < 0.55) {
const f = 1800 + Math.random() * 1400;
this._tone(f, 0.06, 'sine', this.ambGain, 0.018);
this._tone(f * 1.12, 0.05, 'sine', this.ambGain, 0.012, 0.07);
}
this._timers.push(setTimeout(bird, 1800 + Math.random() * 4200));
};
bird();
const city = () => {
if (!this.unlocked) return;
const hornChance = this._floodHorns ? 0.75 : 0.35;
if (Math.random() < hornChance) this._tone(280 + Math.random() * 100, 0.14, 'square', this.ambGain, this._floodHorns ? 0.028 : 0.014);
if (Math.random() < 0.25) this._noiseBurst(0.08, 0.015, this.ambGain, 1600);
this._timers.push(setTimeout(city, this._floodHorns ? 1600 + Math.random() * 2200 : 3500 + Math.random() * 5500));
};
city();
}
_startMusic() {
if (this._buffers.music) {
this._playBuffer('music', this.musicGain, { loop: true, vol: 0.55 });
return;
}
const scale = [196, 220, 247, 294, 330, 392, 440];
const pattern = [0, 2, 4, 2, 5, 4, 3, 1, 0, 4, 2, 5];
const tick = () => {
if (!this.unlocked || !this.ctx) return;
const note = scale[pattern[this._musicStep % pattern.length]];
this._tone(note, 0.55, 'triangle', this.musicGain, 0.028);
this._tone(note * 2, 0.4, 'sine', this.musicGain, 0.012, 0.08);
if (this._musicStep % 4 === 0) this._tone(note * 0.5, 0.7, 'sine', this.musicGain, 0.016);
this._musicStep++;
this._timers.push(setTimeout(tick, 720));
};
tick();
}
dispose() {
for (const t of this._timers) clearTimeout(t);
this._timers.length = 0;
for (const n of this._bedNodes) { try { n.stop(); } catch (_) {} }
this._bedNodes.length = 0;
if (this.ctx) { try { this.ctx.close(); } catch (_) {} }
this.ctx = null;
this.unlocked = false;
}
}

/* ── Game loop ── */
class GameLoop {
constructor({ update, render, onFps }) {
this._update = update;
this._render = render;
this._onFps = onFps;
this._running = false;
this._pausedLogic = false;
this._accumulator = 0;
this._lastTime = 0;
this._rafId = 0;
this._fpsFrames = 0;
this._fpsWindowStart = 0;
this._fpsEl = document.getElementById('fps-meter');
this._tick = this._tick.bind(this);
}
start() {
if (this._running) return;
this._running = true;
this._lastTime = performance.now();
this._fpsWindowStart = this._lastTime;
this._rafId = requestAnimationFrame(this._tick);
}
stop() {
this._running = false;
cancelAnimationFrame(this._rafId);
}
setLogicPaused(v) { this._pausedLogic = v; }
_tick(now) {
if (!this._running) return;
const step = GameConfig.loop.fixedStep;
let frameDelta = (now - this._lastTime) / 1000;
this._lastTime = now;
if (frameDelta > GameConfig.loop.maxFrameDelta) frameDelta = GameConfig.loop.maxFrameDelta;
if (!this._pausedLogic && !document.hidden) {
this._accumulator += frameDelta;
while (this._accumulator >= step) {
this._update(step);
this._accumulator -= step;
}
} else {
this._accumulator = 0;
}
this._render(this._accumulator / step);
this._fpsFrames++;
if (now - this._fpsWindowStart >= 500) {
const fps = Math.round(this._fpsFrames / ((now - this._fpsWindowStart) / 1000));
if (this._fpsEl) this._fpsEl.textContent = fps + ' FPS';
if (this._onFps) this._onFps(fps);
this._fpsFrames = 0;
this._fpsWindowStart = now;
}
this._rafId = requestAnimationFrame(this._tick);
}
}

/* ── Scene manager ── */
class SceneManager {
constructor(bus, hostEl, settings) {
this.bus = bus;
this.hostEl = hostEl;
this.settings = settings;
this.scene = new THREE.Scene();
this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(GameConfig.world.fogColor, GameConfig.world.fogNear, GameConfig.world.fogFar);
this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x87c6d8);

const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl2') || canvas.getContext('webgl');
if (!gl) {
this.unsupported = true;
return;
}
this.unsupported = false;
const tierName = settings.getResolvedQuality();
const tier = GameConfig.qualityTiers[tierName];
this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
canvas, antialias: tier.antialias, powerPreference: 'high-performance',
alpha: false,
});
this.renderer.setClearColor(0x87c6d8, 1);
this.renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
this.renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
this.renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
this.renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
this.renderer.domElement.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
this.renderer.domElement.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Game canvas');
hostEl.appendChild(this.renderer.domElement);

this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(46, 1, 0.1, 320);
this._menuCamT = 0;
this._camTarget = new THREE.Vector3();
this._applyRig(this._cameraRig(), 0);
this._resizeQueued = false;
this._onResize = () => {
if (this._resizeQueued) return;
this._resizeQueued = true;
requestAnimationFrame(() => {
this._resizeQueued = false;
this._doResize();
});
};
window.addEventListener('resize', this._onResize);
window.addEventListener('orientationchange', this._onResize);
this._doResize();

this.renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', (e) => {
e.preventDefault();
this.bus.emit('webgl:contextlost');
});
this.renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', () => {
this.bus.emit('webgl:contextrestored');
});

bus.on('settings:graphics', (name) => this.applyQuality(name));
this.applyQuality(tierName);
}
applyQuality(tierName) {
const tier = GameConfig.qualityTiers[tierName] || GameConfig.qualityTiers.medium;
this.currentTier = tier;
this.currentTierName = tierName;
this.renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, tier.pixelRatioCap));
this.renderer.shadowMap.enabled = !!tier.shadows;
this.bus.emit('quality:applied', tier);
this._doResize();
}
_doResize() {
const w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
this.renderer.setSize(w, h, true);
this.camera.aspect = w / h;
this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
this.bus.emit('viewport:resized', { width: w, height: h, aspect: w / h });
}
add(obj) { this.scene.add(obj); }
remove(obj) { this.scene.remove(obj); }
render() { this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera); }
// Cinematic 3/4 rig: city bank on the left, open bay on the right, bin low-right.
// Distance is solved from the horizontal FOV so the whole canal fits any aspect.
_cameraRig(driftYaw = 0, driftLift = 0) {
const aspect = this.camera ? this.camera.aspect : 16 / 9;
const portrait = aspect < 0.95;
const vHalf = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(46) / 2;
const hHalf = Math.atan(Math.tan(vHalf) * aspect);
if (portrait) {
return { yaw: -0.52 + driftYaw, elev: 0.62, dist: 34, target: new THREE.Vector3(4.0, 0.45, 1.2), lift: driftLift };
}
const dist = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(19.5 / Math.tan(hHalf), 23, 36);
return { yaw: 0.08 + driftYaw, elev: 0.45, dist, target: new THREE.Vector3(2.4, 0.4, 0.2), lift: driftLift };
}
_applyRig(rig, smooth = 0) {
const { yaw, elev, dist, target } = rig;
const cx = target.x + Math.sin(yaw) * Math.cos(elev) * dist;
const cy = target.y + Math.sin(elev) * dist + (rig.lift || 0);
const cz = target.z + Math.cos(yaw) * Math.cos(elev) * dist;
if (smooth > 0) {
this.camera.position.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(cx, cy, cz), smooth);
} else {
this.camera.position.set(cx, cy, cz);
}
this._camTarget.copy(target);
this.camera.lookAt(target);
}
updateCamera(dt, mode, reducedMotion) {
if (mode === 'menu') {
this._menuCamT += reducedMotion ? 0 : dt;
const yaw = Math.sin(this._menuCamT * 0.16) * 0.07;
const lift = Math.sin(this._menuCamT * 0.21) * 0.35;
this._applyRig(this._cameraRig(yaw, lift), 0.08);
} else if (mode === 'play') {
this._applyRig(this._cameraRig(), 0.12);
}
}
dispose() {
window.removeEventListener('resize', this._onResize);
window.removeEventListener('orientationchange', this._onResize);
this.renderer.dispose();
if (this.renderer.domElement.parentNode) {
this.renderer.domElement.parentNode.removeChild(this.renderer.domElement);
}
}
}

/* ── Shared helpers ── */
function seededRand(seedObj) {
seedObj.s = (seedObj.s * 16807) % 2147483647;
return seedObj.s / 2147483647;
}
function disposeObject3D(root) {
root.traverse((obj) => {
if (obj.geometry) obj.geometry.dispose();
if (obj.material) {
const mats = Array.isArray(obj.material) ? obj.material : [obj.material];
for (const m of mats) {
if (m.map) m.map.dispose();
m.dispose();
}
}
});
}

/* ── Environment ── */
class EnvironmentManager {
constructor(bus, sceneManager) {
this.bus = bus;
this.sceneManager = sceneManager;
this.group = new THREE.Group();
this.group.name = 'Environment';
this._time = 0;
this._seed = { s: 20260725 };
this._clouds = [];
this._birds = [];
this._vehicles = [];
this._pedestrians = [];
this._waterMats = [];
this._riverTrash = [];
this._particles = [];
this.pollution = 0;
this.binMesh = null;
this.binGlow = null;
this.binLid = null;
this.dropVolume = new THREE.Box3();
this._throwCallbacks = [];
this._throwArcs = [];
this.shared = {};
this.jam = 0;
this.waterRise = 0;
this.flooding = false;
this._forceFlood = false;
this.rainIntensity = 0;
this.cloudDensity = 0.35;
this.vapor = 0;
this._rainDrops = [];
this._vaporPuffs = [];
this._speechTimers = [];
this._bridgeX = LevelConfig.bridgeX;
this._netGroup = null;
this._baseWaterY = LevelConfig.canal.waterY;
}
_r() { return seededRand(this._seed); }

async buildProgressive(onProgress) {
const steps = [
['buildingCanal', () => this._buildCanal()],
['buildingCity', () => this._buildCity()],
['buildingTraffic', () => this._buildTrafficAndPeople()],
['buildingWater', () => { this._buildMarineLife(); this._buildAtmosphere(); }],
['warmingGpu', () => this._finalize()],
];
for (let i = 0; i < steps.length; i++) {
const [key, fn] = steps[i];
onProgress((i) / steps.length, L10N.t(key));
fn();
await new Promise(requestAnimationFrame);
await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 420));
}
onProgress(1, L10N.t('ready'));
this.sceneManager.add(this.group);
}

_mat(color, opts = {}) {
return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
color, roughness: opts.roughness ?? 0.85, metalness: opts.metalness ?? 0.05,
transparent: !!opts.transparent, opacity: opts.opacity ?? 1,
emissive: opts.emissive ? new THREE.Color(opts.emissive) : new THREE.Color(0x000000),
emissiveIntensity: opts.emissiveIntensity ?? 0,
});
}

_box(w, h, d, mat, x, y, z, parent = this.group) {
const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), mat);
m.position.set(x, y, z);
m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true;
parent.add(m);
return m;
}

_buildLighting() {
// Cool daylight Dhaka — avoid warm sepia; dirt only arrives with pollution
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcfe8f4, 0x5a6a58, 0.95);
const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff4e6, 1.15);
sun.position.set(42, 36, 14);
sun.castShadow = true;
sun.shadow.camera.left = -36;
sun.shadow.camera.right = 36;
sun.shadow.camera.top = 36;
sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -36;
sun.shadow.camera.far = 150;
sun.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024);
sun.shadow.bias = -0.001;
const fill = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x9eb8d0, 0.42);
fill.position.set(-18, 14, -12);
const bounce = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x8aa898, 0.22);
bounce.position.set(8, 6, 20);
this.sun = sun;
this.hemi = hemi;
this.group.add(hemi, sun, sun.target, fill, bounce);
this.sceneManager.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(GameConfig.world.fogColor, GameConfig.world.fogNear, GameConfig.world.fogFar);
this.sceneManager.scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x8eb8c8);
this.bus.on('quality:applied', (tier) => {
sun.castShadow = !!tier.shadows;
sun.shadow.mapSize.set(tier.shadowMapSize, tier.shadowMapSize);
if (sun.shadow.map) { sun.shadow.map.dispose(); sun.shadow.map = null; }
this._pedestrians.forEach((p, i) => { p.visible = i < tier.pedestrians; });
// Keep enough cars visible so flood jams still read on low/medium
this._trafficCap = Math.max(5, tier.traffic);
this._syncTrafficVisibility();
this._particles.forEach((p, i) => { p.visible = i < tier.particles; });
this._rainDrops.forEach((p, i) => { p.visible = i < (tier.rain || 0) && this.rainIntensity > 0.15; });
});
}

_buildCanal() {
this._buildLighting();
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const halfW = C.width / 2;
const len = C.mouthX - C.westX;
const midX = (C.mouthX + C.westX) / 2;
const bankY = C.bankTopY;

this._buildSky();
this._buildBay();

const concrete = this._surfaceMat('concrete', 0x8d8f8a, 20, 1, { roughness: 0.96 });
const wetConcrete = this._mat(0x574f42, { roughness: 0.88 });
const silt = this._mat(0x3f3826, { roughness: 1 });

// silt bed + retaining walls
this._box(len, 0.3, C.width + 1.2, silt, midX, 0.05, 0);
for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
this._box(len, bankY + 0.35, 0.5, concrete, midX, (bankY + 0.35) / 2 - 0.2, side * (halfW + 0.25));
// tide stain just above the waterline
const stain = this._box(len, 0.5, 0.06, wetConcrete, midX, C.waterY + 0.24, side * (halfW + 0.02));
stain.castShadow = false;
}

// canal water — murky Dhaka drain surface flowing toward +X
const waterGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(len, C.width - 0.1, 108, 24);
waterGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
const waterTex = this._makeWaterNoiseTexture();
const waterMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
color: 0x3a3428, roughness: 0.62, metalness: 0.12,
transparent: true, opacity: 0.97, map: waterTex, envMapIntensity: 0.3,
});
this.waterMesh = new THREE.Mesh(waterGeo, waterMat);
this.waterMesh.position.set(midX, C.waterY, 0);
this.waterMesh.receiveShadow = true;
this.group.add(this.waterMesh);
this._waterMats.push(waterMat);
this._canalTex = waterTex;
this._waterBasePositions = waterGeo.attributes.position.array.slice();

// scum rafts drifting downstream
const foamMat = this._mat(0x6a6555, { transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, roughness: 0.95 });
for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
const f = this._box(1.5, 0.03, C.width * 0.38, foamMat,
C.westX + i * 3.3 + this._r() * 2, C.waterY + 0.04, (this._r() - 0.5) * C.width * 0.55);
f.castShadow = false;
this._clouds.push({ mesh: f, kind: 'foam', speed: 0.55 + this._r() * 0.5 });
}
for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
const edge = this._box(len - 2, 0.03, 0.35, foamMat, midX, C.waterY + 0.045, side * (halfW - 0.35));
edge.castShadow = false;
}

// ── near bank (camera side): footpath + municipal road that carries the bin ──
const pavement = this._surfaceMat('paving', 0x9d968a, 26, 3, { roughness: 0.97 });
const asphalt = this._surfaceMat('asphalt', 0x4a4d52, 18, 2, { roughness: 0.95 });
const promStartX = C.westX - 6;
const promLen = C.promenadeEndX - promStartX;
const promMid = (C.promenadeEndX + promStartX) / 2;
this._box(promLen, bankY, 3.6, pavement, promMid, bankY / 2, halfW + 2.3); // footpath
this._box(promLen, 0.14, 0.5, this._mat(0xe8e2d2), promMid, bankY + 0.06, halfW + 4.2); // kerb
this._box(promLen, bankY, 5.4, asphalt, promMid, bankY / 2 - 0.06, halfW + 7.1); // road
for (let x = promStartX + 1; x < C.promenadeEndX; x += 3.2) { // lane dashes
const d = this._box(1.6, 0.02, 0.16, this._mat(0xd8cf9a), x, bankY + 0.02, halfW + 7.1);
d.castShadow = false;
}
this._box(promLen, bankY, 6, pavement, promMid, bankY / 2 - 0.1, halfW + 12.6); // back lot
this._box(promLen, 0.9, 0.35, this._mat(0x7d7a70), promMid, bankY + 0.45, halfW + 15.4);
// sea wall closing the promenade at the bay end
this._box(0.6, bankY + 0.7, 15, concrete, C.promenadeEndX, (bankY + 0.7) / 2 - 0.1, halfW + 7);

// ── far bank (city side) stops at cityEndX so the bay opens on the right ──
const farStartX = C.westX - 6;
const farLen = C.cityEndX - farStartX;
const farMid = (C.cityEndX + farStartX) / 2;
this._box(farLen, bankY, 22, this._surfaceMat('ground', 0x7d766a, 14, 8, { roughness: 0.98 }), farMid, bankY / 2, -(halfW + 11.5));
this._box(farLen, 0.12, 5.6, asphalt, farMid, bankY + 0.05, -(halfW + 6.6));
// rip-rap headland where the city bank meets open water
for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
const s = 0.5 + this._r() * 0.8;
const rock = this._box(s, s * 0.7, s, this._mat(0x6b6660, { roughness: 1 }),
C.cityEndX + this._r() * 3.4, 0.2 + this._r() * 0.7, -(halfW + 1 + this._r() * 12));
rock.rotation.set(this._r(), this._r() * 3, this._r() * 0.4);
rock.castShadow = false;
}

// railings: full length near side, city stretch on the far side
const rail = this._mat(0x39434c, { metalness: 0.5, roughness: 0.6 });
for (let x = promStartX + 1; x <= C.promenadeEndX - 1; x += 2.4) {
this._box(0.08, 0.95, 0.08, rail, x, bankY + 0.47, halfW + 0.55);
}
this._box(promLen - 2, 0.07, 0.07, rail, promMid, bankY + 0.92, halfW + 0.55);
this._box(promLen - 2, 0.05, 0.05, rail, promMid, bankY + 0.55, halfW + 0.55);
for (let x = farStartX + 1; x <= C.cityEndX - 1; x += 2.4) {
this._box(0.08, 0.95, 0.08, rail, x, bankY + 0.47, -(halfW + 0.55));
}
this._box(farLen - 2, 0.07, 0.07, rail, farMid, bankY + 0.92, -(halfW + 0.55));

// Foot-over-bridge: people cross the drain on this deck
const bx = this._bridgeX;
this._box(2.8, 0.22, C.width + 4.2, concrete, bx, bankY + 1.55, 0);
for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
this._box(2.8, 0.85, 0.12, rail, bx, bankY + 2.0, side * (halfW + 1.7));
this._box(0.4, 1.55, 0.4, concrete, bx, bankY + 0.78, side * (halfW + 1.55));
// approach stairs
for (let s = 0; s < 3; s++) {
this._box(1.1, 0.18, 0.55, concrete, bx, bankY + 0.2 + s * 0.35, side * (halfW + 0.35 + s * 0.55));
}
}

// outfall pipes: the drains that feed the canal (waste enters at spawnX)
this._buildOutfall(C.spawnX, -1);
this._buildOutfall(C.spawnX + 8.5, -1);
this._buildOutfall(C.spawnX + 3.5, 1);

// sluice piers + trash nets at the mouth (block waste from the ocean)
for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
this._box(1.6, 2.6, 1.4, concrete, C.mouthX, 1.1, side * (halfW + 0.6));
this._box(0.5, 1.9, 0.5, this._mat(0x8a3b2f, { roughness: 0.8 }), C.mouthX, 2.4, side * (halfW + 0.6));
}
this._box(0.5, 0.32, C.width + 2.4, concrete, C.mouthX, 3.2, 0);
this._buildMouthNets();
this._buildTetrapods();
this._buildFloodPlanes();
this._buildBin();
}

// Translucent flood sheets over near/far roads — appear when canal overflows
_buildFloodPlanes() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const halfW = C.width / 2;
const makeMat = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
color: 0x2a453c, transparent: true, opacity: 0, roughness: 0.15, metalness: 0.25,
depthWrite: false, emissive: new THREE.Color(0x143028), emissiveIntensity: 0.18,
});
this._floodMat = makeMat();
this._floodMatFar = makeMat();
this._floodPlanes = [];
const near = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(C.length + 10, 9.5), this._floodMat);
near.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
near.position.set((C.westX + C.promenadeEndX) / 2, C.bankTopY + 0.04, halfW + 4.8);
near.renderOrder = 4;
this.group.add(near);
this._floodPlanes.push(near);
const far = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(C.cityEndX - C.westX + 8, 8), this._floodMatFar);
far.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
far.position.set((C.westX + C.cityEndX) / 2, C.bankTopY + 0.04, -(halfW + 5.8));
far.renderOrder = 4;
this.group.add(far);
this._floodPlanes.push(far);
}

// Trash nets at the canal mouth — catch waste before it reaches the Buriganga.
_buildMouthNets() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const halfW = C.width / 2;
this._netGroup = new THREE.Group();
this._netGroup.name = 'MouthNets';
const netMat = this._mat(0x2f3a42, { metalness: 0.35, roughness: 0.55, transparent: true, opacity: 0.82 });
const poleMat = this._mat(0x6a5538, { roughness: 0.85 });
for (let i = -3; i <= 3; i++) {
const z = (i / 3) * (halfW - 0.35);
const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.06, 0.07, 2.2, 6), poleMat);
pole.position.set(C.mouthX - 0.35, 1.0, z);
pole.castShadow = true;
this._netGroup.add(pole);
}
// mesh panels between poles
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
const z0 = ((i - 3) / 3) * (halfW - 0.35);
const z1 = ((i - 2) / 3) * (halfW - 0.35);
const panel = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.15, 1.7),
netMat
);
panel.position.set(C.mouthX - 0.25, 0.95, (z0 + z1) * 0.5);
// horizontal lattice strips
const strip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 0.04, Math.abs(z1 - z0) + 0.05), netMat);
strip.position.set(C.mouthX - 0.22, 0.55 + (i % 3) * 0.35, (z0 + z1) * 0.5);
this._netGroup.add(panel, strip);
}
// float boom across mouth
const boom = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.14, C.width - 0.8, 4, 8),
this._mat(0xd4543a, { roughness: 0.7 })
);
boom.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
boom.position.set(C.mouthX - 0.55, 0.28, 0);
this._netGroup.add(boom);
this.group.add(this._netGroup);
}

// Concrete tetrapods lining canal→ocean transition (as in the reference)
_buildTetrapods() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const rock = this._mat(0x6e7378, { roughness: 0.95 });
const makeTetra = () => {
const g = new THREE.Group();
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
const arm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22, 0.28, 1.35, 6), rock);
arm.position.y = 0.35;
if (i === 0) arm.rotation.z = 0.9;
if (i === 1) arm.rotation.z = -0.9;
if (i === 2) { arm.rotation.x = 0.9; }
if (i === 3) { arm.rotation.x = -0.9; }
g.add(arm);
}
return g;
};
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
const t = makeTetra();
t.position.set(
C.mouthX + 1.2 + (i % 5) * 1.15 + this._r() * 0.2,
0.15,
-2.2 - Math.floor(i / 5) * 2.4 - this._r() * 0.8
);
t.rotation.y = this._r() * Math.PI;
t.scale.setScalar(0.85 + this._r() * 0.35);
this.group.add(t);
}
}

// Big open water plane: everything not covered by land reads as the Buriganga mouth / bay.
_buildBay() {
const bayTex = this._makeWaterNoiseTexture();
bayTex.repeat.set(16, 14);
const bayMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
color: 0x39584f, roughness: 0.34, metalness: 0.26,
map: bayTex, transparent: true, opacity: 0.98, envMapIntensity: 0.6,
});
const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(230, 180, 28, 20);
geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
this.oceanMesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, bayMat);
this.oceanMesh.position.set(72, 0.05, -72);
this.oceanMesh.receiveShadow = true;
this.oceanMesh.renderOrder = 2;
this.group.add(this.oceanMesh);

// silt bed under the bay so clear water reveals depth, not empty space
const bed = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(230, 180),
this._surfaceMat('ground', 0x4d4634, 20, 16, { roughness: 1 })
);
bed.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
bed.position.set(72, -2.1, -72);
bed.receiveShadow = true;
this.group.add(bed);
this._waterMats.push(bayMat);
this._bayTex = bayTex;
this._oceanBasePositions = geo.attributes.position.array.slice();

// sun glitter band on the bay, strongest toward the light
const glitter = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(70, 90, 1, 1),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
color: 0xffe0a8, transparent: true, opacity: 0.16,
blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,
})
);
glitter.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
glitter.position.set(46, 0.09, -22);
this.group.add(glitter);
this._glitter = glitter;

// far shoreline silhouette so the bay has a horizon edge, not an empty void
const shore = this._box(200, 1.1, 6, this._mat(0x63705f, { roughness: 1 }), 60, 0.4, -96);
shore.castShadow = false;
for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
const h = 1.4 + this._r() * 5;
const b = this._box(1.6 + this._r() * 3, h, 2, this._mat(0x76858c, { roughness: 1 }),
-18 + i * 6.4, 0.9 + h / 2, -95 + this._r() * 3);
b.castShadow = false;
}
// ground behind the near road so the frame never shows a hole
const backLot = this._box(120, 0.4, 60, this._mat(0x6f6a61, { roughness: 1 }), 0, 0.75, 46);
backLot.castShadow = false;
}

_buildOutfall(x, side) {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const halfW = C.width / 2;
const z = side * (halfW - 0.05);
const pipe = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 0.9, 12, 1, true),
this._mat(0x2b2f31, { roughness: 0.95 })
);
pipe.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
pipe.position.set(x, C.waterY + 0.45, z);
pipe.castShadow = false;
const stain = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.5, 0.9),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x2f2a1c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5 })
);
stain.position.set(x, C.waterY + 0.42, z - side * 0.03);
stain.rotation.y = side > 0 ? Math.PI : 0;
const spill = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.1, 2.6),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x5a5232, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35, depthWrite: false })
);
spill.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
spill.position.set(x + 0.9, C.waterY + 0.02, z - side * 1.2);
this.group.add(pipe, stain, spill);
}

// Municipal recycling bin on the near-side footpath: hinged lid that opens
// when the pointer (or a dragged item) comes over it.
_buildBin() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const bin = new THREE.Group();
bin.position.set(C.binX, C.binY != null ? C.binY : 0.95, C.binZ);

const shell = this._mat(0x1f7a44, { roughness: 0.42, metalness: 0.28 });
const shellDark = this._mat(0x13512d, { roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.3 });
const steel = this._mat(0x59636b, { roughness: 0.45, metalness: 0.65 });

const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.74, 0.66, 1.3, 20), shell);
body.position.y = 0.68;
body.castShadow = true; body.receiveShadow = true;
for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) { // moulded ribs
const a = (i / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
const rib = this._box(0.06, 1.1, 0.06, shellDark,
Math.sin(a) * 0.72, 0.68, Math.cos(a) * 0.72, bin);
rib.castShadow = false;
}
const rim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.75, 0.055, 8, 26), shellDark);
rim.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
rim.position.y = 1.33;

// lid hinged at the back edge so it swings open away from the player
const hinge = new THREE.Group();
hinge.position.set(0, 1.36, -0.72);
const lid = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.78, 0.76, 0.11, 20), shellDark);
lid.position.set(0, 0.02, 0.72);
lid.castShadow = true;
const handle = this._box(0.5, 0.07, 0.12, steel, 0, 0.09, 1.34, hinge);
handle.castShadow = false;
hinge.add(lid);
this.binLidPivot = hinge;
this.binLid = lid;
this._lidNow = 0;
this._lidTarget = 0;

// recycling mark facing the player
const mark = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.78, 0.78),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: this._makeRecycleTexture(), transparent: true })
);
mark.position.set(0, 0.78, 0.735);
this.binMark = mark;

const label = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.9, 0.34),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
map: this._makeSignTexture(L10N.t('binLabel'), '#f4f1e8', '#14532d'), transparent: true,
})
);
label.position.set(0, 0.28, 0.7);
this.binSideLabel = label;

// pedal + wheels: reads as real municipal hardware
const pedal = this._box(0.5, 0.06, 0.22, steel, 0, 0.12, 0.7, bin);
pedal.castShadow = false;
for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
const wheel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.14, 0.06, 6, 14), this._mat(0x1c1c1c));
wheel.position.set(s * 0.5, 0.14, -0.42);
wheel.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
bin.add(wheel);
}

// sign post
const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.055, 2.2, 10), steel);
pole.position.set(1.15, 1.1, 0);
pole.castShadow = true;
const board = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.5, 0.6),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
map: this._makeSignTexture(L10N.t('binLabel'), '#14532d', '#f4f1e8'),
transparent: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
})
);
board.position.set(1.15, 2.25, 0);
this.binBoard = board;

const glow = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.5, 16, 12),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
color: 0x5ff0c0, transparent: true, opacity: 0,
blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,
})
);
glow.position.y = 0.7;
this.binGlow = glow;
const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.RingGeometry(1.15, 1.5, 40),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
color: 0x4fe3bd, transparent: true, opacity: 0.24,
side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false,
})
);
ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
ring.position.y = 0.03;
this.binRing = ring;

// invisible, generous hover/drop proxy
const proxy = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.35, 1.35, 2.8, 10),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ visible: false })
);
proxy.position.y = 1.1;
proxy.name = 'binHitProxy';
this.binProxy = proxy;

bin.add(body, rim, hinge, mark, label, pole, board, glow, ring, proxy);
this.group.add(bin);
this.binMesh = bin;
this._updateDropVolume();
this.bus.on('locale:changed', () => this.refreshBinLabels());
}

getBinHitTargets() { return this.binProxy ? [this.binProxy] : []; }

_makeRecycleTexture() {
const c = document.createElement('canvas');
c.width = c.height = 128;
const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
ctx.strokeStyle = '#eaf7ec';
ctx.fillStyle = '#eaf7ec';
ctx.lineWidth = 11;
ctx.lineCap = 'round';
for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
ctx.save();
ctx.translate(64, 66);
ctx.rotate((i * Math.PI * 2) / 3);
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.moveTo(-24, -14);
ctx.lineTo(6, -34);
ctx.stroke();
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.moveTo(6, -42);
ctx.lineTo(22, -28);
ctx.lineTo(2, -20);
ctx.closePath();
ctx.fill();
ctx.restore();
}
const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
return tex;
}

refreshBinLabels() {
const text = L10N.t('binLabel');
if (this.binSideLabel && this.binSideLabel.material.map) {
this.binSideLabel.material.map.dispose();
this.binSideLabel.material.map = this._makeSignTexture(text, '#f4f1e8', '#14532d');
this.binSideLabel.material.needsUpdate = true;
}
if (this.binBoard && this.binBoard.material.map) {
this.binBoard.material.map.dispose();
this.binBoard.material.map = this._makeSignTexture(text, '#14532d', '#f4f1e8');
this.binBoard.material.needsUpdate = true;
}
}

_updateDropVolume() {
if (!this.binMesh) return;
this.dropVolume.setFromCenterAndSize(
this.binMesh.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.0, 0)),
new THREE.Vector3(3.4, 3.2, 3.4)
);
}

isOverBin(pos) {
if (!this.binMesh) return false;
if (this.dropVolume.containsPoint(pos)) return true;
const r = LevelConfig.canal.binAssistRadius;
const dx = pos.x - this.binMesh.position.x;
const dz = pos.z - this.binMesh.position.z;
return (dx * dx + dz * dz) <= r * r;
}

_makeSignTexture(text, bg, fg) {
const c = document.createElement('canvas');
c.width = 256; c.height = 96;
const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
ctx.fillStyle = bg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 96);
ctx.fillStyle = fg;
ctx.font = 'bold 36px Hind Siliguri, Noto Sans KR, Nirmala UI, Malgun Gothic, sans-serif';
ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
ctx.fillText(text, 128, 48);
const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
return tex;
}

_makeWaterNoiseTexture() {
const c = document.createElement('canvas');
c.width = 256; c.height = 256;
const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
const img = ctx.createImageData(256, 256);
for (let y = 0; y < 256; y++) {
for (let x = 0; x < 256; x++) {
const i = (y * 256 + x) * 4;
const n = (Math.sin(x * 0.13) * Math.cos(y * 0.10) + Math.sin((x + y) * 0.06)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
const m = (Math.sin(x * 0.5 + y * 0.3) * 0.5 + 0.5);
const v = 34 + Math.floor(n * 46 + m * 10);
// murky brown-green sludge
img.data[i] = v * 0.9;
img.data[i + 1] = v * 0.85;
img.data[i + 2] = v * 0.55;
img.data[i + 3] = 255;
}
}
ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
// dark scum blotches
for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(20,22,14,${0.08 + Math.random() * 0.14})`;
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.ellipse(Math.random() * 256, Math.random() * 256, 6 + Math.random() * 22, 4 + Math.random() * 12, Math.random() * 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
ctx.fill();
}
// thin oily rainbow film streaks
const oil = ['rgba(120,90,140,0.10)', 'rgba(90,130,120,0.10)', 'rgba(150,120,70,0.10)'];
for (let i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
ctx.strokeStyle = oil[i % oil.length];
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.moveTo(Math.random() * 256, Math.random() * 256);
ctx.quadraticCurveTo(Math.random() * 256, Math.random() * 256, Math.random() * 256, Math.random() * 256);
ctx.lineWidth = 1 + Math.random() * 2.5;
ctx.stroke();
}
const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
tex.wrapS = tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
tex.repeat.set(6, 2);
tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
return tex;
}

// ── Procedural surface library ────────────────────────────────────────────
// Canvas textures keep the build offline-safe (no CORS, no downloads) while
// giving concrete, asphalt, paving and monsoon-stained plaster real grain.
_surfaceCanvas(kind) {
this._surfaceCache = this._surfaceCache || {};
if (this._surfaceCache[kind]) return this._surfaceCache[kind];
const c = document.createElement('canvas');
c.width = c.height = 256;
const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
const rnd = () => Math.random();
if (kind === 'concrete') {
ctx.fillStyle = '#a6a49c'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
for (let i = 0; i < 2600; i++) {
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${90 + rnd() * 90},${90 + rnd() * 90},${85 + rnd() * 80},0.08)`;
ctx.fillRect(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 2 + rnd() * 3, 1 + rnd() * 3);
}
for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { // damp streaks
ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(70,68,58,${0.05 + rnd() * 0.12})`;
ctx.lineWidth = 3 + rnd() * 12;
ctx.beginPath();
const x = rnd() * 256;
ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x + (rnd() - 0.5) * 20, 256);
ctx.stroke();
}
for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { // form-work seams
ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(60,58,52,0.25)';
ctx.lineWidth = 1;
const y = rnd() * 256;
ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(256, y); ctx.stroke();
}
} else if (kind === 'asphalt') {
ctx.fillStyle = '#43464a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
for (let i = 0; i < 5200; i++) {
const v = 40 + rnd() * 80;
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${v},${v + 2},${v + 6},0.5)`;
ctx.fillRect(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 1 + rnd() * 2, 1 + rnd() * 2);
}
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { // patched repairs
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(30,32,34,${0.1 + rnd() * 0.2})`;
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.ellipse(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 10 + rnd() * 30, 8 + rnd() * 20, rnd() * 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
ctx.fill();
}
} else if (kind === 'paving') {
ctx.fillStyle = '#b4ac9d'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
const s = 64;
for (let y = 0; y < 256; y += s) {
for (let x = 0; x < 256; x += s) {
const off = (y / s) % 2 ? s / 2 : 0;
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${150 + rnd() * 30},${142 + rnd() * 28},${126 + rnd() * 26},1)`;
ctx.fillRect(x + off + 1.5, y + 1.5, s - 3, s - 3);
}
}
for (let i = 0; i < 500; i++) {
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(90,84,72,${rnd() * 0.16})`;
ctx.fillRect(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 3 + rnd() * 6, 3 + rnd() * 6);
}
} else if (kind === 'plaster') {
ctx.fillStyle = '#d8d2c4'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
for (let i = 0; i < 1800; i++) {
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${150 + rnd() * 90},${145 + rnd() * 85},${130 + rnd() * 80},0.10)`;
ctx.fillRect(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 3 + rnd() * 6, 3 + rnd() * 6);
}
for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) { // monsoon run-off stains
const x = rnd() * 256;
const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x, 256);
g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(88,92,72,0.30)');
g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(88,92,72,0)');
ctx.fillStyle = g;
ctx.fillRect(x, 0, 4 + rnd() * 14, 256);
}
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { // moss patches near the base
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(78,96,62,${0.05 + rnd() * 0.12})`;
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.ellipse(rnd() * 256, 200 + rnd() * 56, 12 + rnd() * 26, 6 + rnd() * 14, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
ctx.fill();
}
} else { // dusty ground
ctx.fillStyle = '#8a8071'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
for (let i = 0; i < 4200; i++) {
const v = 90 + rnd() * 80;
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${v},${v - 8},${v - 26},0.35)`;
ctx.fillRect(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 1 + rnd() * 4, 1 + rnd() * 3);
}
for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(60,56,48,${0.08 + rnd() * 0.14})`;
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.ellipse(rnd() * 256, rnd() * 256, 4 + rnd() * 16, 3 + rnd() * 9, rnd() * 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
ctx.fill();
}
}
this._surfaceCache[kind] = c;
return c;
}

_surfaceTex(kind, rx = 4, ry = 4) {
const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this._surfaceCanvas(kind));
tex.wrapS = tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
tex.repeat.set(rx, ry);
tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
tex.anisotropy = 4;
return tex;
}

_surfaceMat(kind, color, rx, ry, opts = {}) {
const m = this._mat(color, opts);
m.map = this._surfaceTex(kind, rx, ry);
return m;
}

_buildSky() {
const c = document.createElement('canvas');
c.width = 8; c.height = 256;
const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 256);
g.addColorStop(0.00, '#1e4f78');
g.addColorStop(0.35, '#4a8fb5');
g.addColorStop(0.58, '#8ebfd0');
g.addColorStop(0.78, '#b8d4e0');
g.addColorStop(1.00, '#9ab8b8');
ctx.fillStyle = g;
ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 256);
const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
const sky = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.SphereGeometry(220, 24, 18),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: tex, side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false, depthWrite: false })
);
sky.name = 'sky';
sky.frustumCulled = false;
this.group.add(sky);
this.skyMesh = sky;
}

// ── Marine life: the reward for cleaning the river ────────────────────────
_makeFish(color, size) {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(size * 0.34, size * 1.5, 8), matStd(color, { roughness: 0.35, metalness: 0.25 }));
body.rotation.z = -Math.PI / 2;
body.scale.set(1, 1, 0.55);
const tail = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.ConeGeometry(size * 0.32, size * 0.5, 3),
matStd(color, { roughness: 0.4 })
);
tail.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
tail.position.x = -size * 0.9;
tail.scale.set(1, 1, 0.35);
const fin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(size * 0.2, size * 0.4, 3), matStd(color, { roughness: 0.5 }));
fin.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
fin.position.set(0, size * 0.3, 0);
g.add(body, tail, fin);
g.userData.tail = tail;
return g;
}

_makeOctopus() {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const skin = matStd(0x8e4b6b, { roughness: 0.6 });
const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.55, 12, 10), skin);
head.scale.set(1, 1.25, 1);
const eyeMat = matStd(0xf6f2e6, { roughness: 0.3 });
for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
const eye = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.12, 8, 8), eyeMat);
eye.position.set(s * 0.25, 0.18, 0.46);
g.add(eye);
const pupil = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.055, 6, 6), matStd(0x181014));
pupil.position.set(s * 0.26, 0.18, 0.55);
g.add(pupil);
}
const arms = [];
for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
const arm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.08, 0.8, 3, 6), skin);
arm.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 0.33, -0.55, Math.sin(a) * 0.33);
arm.rotation.z = Math.cos(a) * 0.5;
arm.rotation.x = -Math.sin(a) * 0.5;
arm.userData.base = { a, x: arm.rotation.x, z: arm.rotation.z };
arms.push(arm);
g.add(arm);
}
g.add(head);
g.userData.arms = arms;
return g;
}

// Ganges river dolphin (shushuk) — Bangladesh's national aquatic mammal.
_makeDolphin() {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const skin = matStd(0x8d9aa6, { roughness: 0.35, metalness: 0.1 });
const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.36, 1.5, 5, 10), skin);
body.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
const snout = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.14, 0.8, 8), skin);
snout.rotation.z = -Math.PI / 2;
snout.position.x = 1.2;
const dorsal = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.16, 0.34, 4), skin);
dorsal.position.set(-0.1, 0.42, 0);
const fluke = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.42, 0.5, 4), skin);
fluke.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
fluke.scale.set(1, 1, 0.25);
fluke.position.x = -1.15;
g.add(body, snout, dorsal, fluke);
return g;
}

_buildMarineLife() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
this._marine = [];
this.marineGroup = new THREE.Group();
this.marineGroup.name = 'MarineLife';
this.group.add(this.marineGroup);

const fishColors = [0xc9d6db, 0xd6a25a, 0x9ec7b8, 0xb87d6a, 0xdad2a8];
for (let s = 0; s < 5; s++) { // schools circling in the bay
const cx = C.mouthX + 8 + this._r() * 26;
const cz = -6 - this._r() * 30;
const radius = 2.4 + this._r() * 3.4;
const speed = 0.45 + this._r() * 0.4;
const color = fishColors[s % fishColors.length];
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
const size = 0.42 + this._r() * 0.3;
const fish = this._makeFish(color, size);
this.marineGroup.add(fish);
this._marine.push({
mesh: fish, kind: 'fish', cx, cz, radius: radius + this._r() * 0.8,
phase: (i / 6) * Math.PI * 2 + this._r() * 0.5, speed,
depth: -0.35 - this._r() * 0.5, need: 0.35 + s * 0.1,
});
}
}

for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) { // octopus near the sluice rocks
const oct = this._makeOctopus();
this.marineGroup.add(oct);
this._marine.push({
mesh: oct, kind: 'octopus',
cx: C.mouthX + 5 + this._r() * 12, cz: -3 - this._r() * 12,
radius: 1.6 + this._r() * 1.6, phase: this._r() * 6, speed: 0.16,
depth: -0.55, need: 0.6,
});
}

const dolphin = this._makeDolphin(); // shushuk breaches only when clean
this.marineGroup.add(dolphin);
this._marine.push({
mesh: dolphin, kind: 'dolphin',
cx: C.mouthX + 16, cz: -14, radius: 9, phase: 0, speed: 0.22, depth: -0.4, need: 0.72,
});

this._marine.forEach((m) => { m.mesh.visible = false; });
this._applyMarineVisibility();
}

_applyMarineVisibility() {
if (!this._marine) return;
const cleanliness = 1 - this.pollution / 100;
for (const m of this._marine) {
m.mesh.visible = cleanliness >= m.need;
}
}

_updateMarineLife(dt, t) {
if (!this._marine) return;
for (const m of this._marine) {
if (!m.mesh.visible) continue;
m.phase += dt * m.speed;
if (m.kind === 'fish') {
const x = m.cx + Math.cos(m.phase) * m.radius;
const z = m.cz + Math.sin(m.phase) * m.radius * 0.7;
m.mesh.position.set(x, m.depth + Math.sin(t * 1.4 + m.phase) * 0.12, z);
m.mesh.rotation.y = -m.phase + Math.PI / 2;
if (m.mesh.userData.tail) m.mesh.userData.tail.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 9 + m.phase) * 0.5;
} else if (m.kind === 'octopus') {
const x = m.cx + Math.cos(m.phase) * m.radius;
const z = m.cz + Math.sin(m.phase * 0.8) * m.radius;
m.mesh.position.set(x, m.depth + Math.sin(t * 0.9 + m.phase) * 0.22, z);
m.mesh.rotation.y = -m.phase;
const arms = m.mesh.userData.arms || [];
arms.forEach((arm, i) => {
const b = arm.userData.base;
arm.rotation.x = b.x + Math.sin(t * 2.2 + i) * 0.28;
arm.rotation.z = b.z + Math.cos(t * 2.0 + i) * 0.28;
});
} else if (m.kind === 'dolphin') {
const x = m.cx + Math.cos(m.phase) * m.radius;
const z = m.cz + Math.sin(m.phase) * m.radius * 0.6;
// breach on part of the loop, submerge for the rest
const arc = Math.sin(m.phase * 2);
const y = arc > 0 ? Math.pow(arc, 2) * 1.5 - 0.35 : -0.6;
m.mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
m.mesh.rotation.y = -m.phase + Math.PI / 2;
m.mesh.rotation.z = arc > 0 ? Math.cos(m.phase * 2) * 0.5 : 0;
}
}
}

_buildBuilding(x, z, w, d, floors, color) {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const h = floors * 2.4 + 1.2;
if (!this._plasterShared) this._plasterShared = this._surfaceTex('plaster', 1.6, 2.6);
const wall = this._mat(color, { roughness: 0.92 });
wall.map = this._plasterShared;
const body = this._box(w, h, d, wall, 0, h / 2, 0, g);
// shopfront band at street level
this._box(w + 0.06, 1.1, d + 0.06, this._mat(0x4d4a44, { roughness: 0.85 }), 0, 0.55, 0, g);
// windows
const winMat = this._mat(0x9fd7ef, { roughness: 0.3, metalness: 0.4, emissive: 0x224455, emissiveIntensity: 0.15 });
for (let f = 0; f < floors; f++) {
for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(2, Math.floor(w)); i++) {
const wx = -w / 2 + 0.7 + i * ((w - 1.2) / Math.max(1, Math.floor(w) - 1));
const wy = 1.4 + f * 2.4;
const win = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.55, 0.75), winMat);
win.position.set(wx, wy, d / 2 + 0.02);
g.add(win);
}
}
// balcony
if (floors > 2) {
this._box(w * 0.7, 0.08, 0.5, this._mat(0x777b80), 0, h * 0.55, d / 2 + 0.25, g);
}
// roof tank
if (this._r() > 0.35) {
const tank = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.35, 0.35, 0.55, 10), this._mat(0x3a7ca5));
tank.position.set(w * 0.2, h + 0.35, 0);
g.add(tank);
}
// AC unit
if (this._r() > 0.4) {
this._box(0.45, 0.3, 0.35, this._mat(0xcccccc), w / 2 - 0.3, h * 0.4, d / 2 + 0.2, g);
}
// laundry
if (this._r() > 0.5) {
const cloth = this._box(0.8, 0.5, 0.05, this._mat(0xe8a33d), 0, h * 0.7, d / 2 + 0.35, g);
this._clouds.push({ mesh: cloth, kind: 'laundry', phase: this._r() * 6 });
}
// corrugated roof lip
this._box(w + 0.2, 0.12, d + 0.2, this._mat(0x5a6066), 0, h + 0.05, 0, g);
g.position.set(x, LevelConfig.canal.bankTopY, z);
this.group.add(g);
return g;
}

// Old Dhaka occupies the left of the frame only; everything right of
// cityEndX stays open water.
_buildCity() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const halfW = C.width / 2;
const startX = C.westX - 6;
const endX = C.cityEndX;
const bankY = C.bankTopY;
const palette = [0xc9b08a, 0xd2c2a4, 0xb9a48a, 0xcfc3b0, 0xa89078, 0xd8c8b0, 0xbfae95];

// front row facing the canal
let x = startX;
while (x < endX) {
const w = 3.2 + this._r() * 2.6;
const d = 3 + this._r() * 2;
const floors = 2 + Math.floor(this._r() * 5);
this._buildBuilding(x + w / 2, -(halfW + 12 + this._r() * 2.5), w, d, floors,
palette[Math.floor(this._r() * palette.length)]);
x += w + 0.7 + this._r();
}
// deeper row for density
x = startX + 1.5;
while (x < endX) {
const w = 3 + this._r() * 2.4;
const d = 3 + this._r() * 2;
const floors = 3 + Math.floor(this._r() * 5);
this._buildBuilding(x + w / 2, -(halfW + 17 + this._r() * 3), w, d, floors,
palette[Math.floor(this._r() * palette.length)]);
x += w + 0.9 + this._r();
}
// left-edge block that closes the composition upstream
for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
this._buildBuilding(startX - 2 - i * 5.2, -(halfW + 6 + this._r() * 2), 4 + this._r() * 2, 4,
3 + Math.floor(this._r() * 3), palette[Math.floor(this._r() * palette.length)]);
}

// shop awnings + Bengali signage facing the water
const signs = ['কাচ্চি হাউস', 'চিপস', 'করিম কমার্শিয়াল', 'আলম ট্রেডার্স', 'হক জুয়েলার্স', 'কোলা ও পানীয়', 'চা দোকান', 'ফল'];
const shopCount = signs.length;
for (let i = 0; i < shopCount; i++) {
const xx = startX + 3 + i * ((endX - startX - 5) / (shopCount - 1));
const awn = this._box(2.4, 0.08, 1.3, this._mat(i % 2 ? 0xd63b3b : 0x2f8f4e), xx, bankY + 1.6, -(halfW + 9.4));
awn.rotation.x = -0.16;
const tex = this._makeSignTexture(signs[i], i % 2 ? '#1a4d3a' : '#7a1f1f', '#fff8e7');
const sign = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.9, 0.58), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: tex }));
sign.position.set(xx, bankY + 2.25, -(halfW + 9.3));
this.group.add(sign);
}

// overhead cable bundles, the signature Dhaka skyline clutter
const cable = this._mat(0x1c1c1c);
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
const c = this._box(endX - startX, 0.035, 0.035, cable, (startX + endX) / 2, 5.4 + i * 0.22, -(halfW + 8 + i * 1.4));
c.rotation.z = 0.004 * (i - 1);
c.castShadow = false;
}
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
const px = startX + 2 + i * ((endX - startX) / 5);
this._box(0.16, 6.6, 0.16, this._mat(0x5b5045), px, bankY + 3.3, -(halfW + 7.6));
}

// street lamps + trees along the city road
for (let lx = startX + 2; lx <= endX; lx += 7.5) {
this._box(0.12, 3.4, 0.12, this._mat(0x333840), lx, bankY + 1.7, -(halfW + 2.9));
const light = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.18, 8, 8),
new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffe0a0, emissive: 0xffc060, emissiveIntensity: 0.85 }));
light.position.set(lx, bankY + 3.5, -(halfW + 2.9));
this.group.add(light);
}
for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
const xx = startX + 2.5 + i * ((endX - startX) / 7);
const trunk = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.17, 1.5, 6), this._mat(0x6b4a2e));
trunk.position.set(xx, bankY + 0.75, -(halfW + 1.7));
const canopy = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.9, 9, 7), this._mat(0x3f8f45));
canopy.position.set(xx, bankY + 1.9, -(halfW + 1.7));
canopy.scale.set(1, 0.8, 1);
canopy.castShadow = true;
this.group.add(trunk, canopy);
this._clouds.push({ mesh: canopy, kind: 'tree', phase: this._r() * 5 });
}

// tea stall + fruit cart
this._box(2.2, 1.7, 1.7, this._mat(0x8b5a2b), -14, bankY + 0.85, -(halfW + 2.3));
this._box(1.7, 0.95, 1.1, this._mat(0xc45c26), -4, bankY + 0.5, -(halfW + 2.1));
const fruitColors = [0xe74c3c, 0xf1c40f, 0x2ecc71];
for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
const fr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.16, 7, 6), this._mat(fruitColors[i]));
fr.position.set(-4.3 + i * 0.28, bankY + 1.05, -(halfW + 2.1));
this.group.add(fr);
}

// waterfront street furniture on the near promenade
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
const bx = C.westX + 4 + i * 8;
if (bx > C.promenadeEndX - 3) break;
this._box(1.5, 0.1, 0.45, this._mat(0x7a5f3c), bx, bankY + 0.45, halfW + 2.4);
this._box(0.12, 0.4, 0.4, this._mat(0x4a4f55), bx - 0.55, bankY + 0.2, halfW + 2.4);
this._box(0.12, 0.4, 0.4, this._mat(0x4a4f55), bx + 0.55, bankY + 0.2, halfW + 2.4);
}

// distant Dhaka skyline behind the city block
for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
const h = 5 + this._r() * 14;
const b = this._box(2 + this._r() * 2.4, h, 2.4, this._mat(0x7a8a96, { roughness: 1 }),
startX + i * 2.6, 0.9 + h / 2, -(halfW + 26 + this._r() * 6));
b.castShadow = false;
}

// upstream closure so the canal does not read as an open cut
this._box(0.4, 3.2, C.width + 26, this._mat(0x9a9080, { roughness: 1 }), C.westX - 4.5, 1.6, -4);
}

_makePedestrian(color) {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const skin = this._mat(0xc79466);
const legMat = this._mat(0x2c3138);
const hairMat = this._mat(0x231a12, { roughness: 0.85 });
const torso = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.16, 0.32, 4, 8), this._mat(color));
torso.position.y = 0.98;
torso.castShadow = true;
const hips = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.15, 0.13, 0.16, 8), legMat);
hips.position.y = 0.74;
const legGeo = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.062, 0.48, 3, 6);
const legL = new THREE.Group();
const legLMesh = new THREE.Mesh(legGeo, legMat);
legLMesh.position.y = -0.28;
legLMesh.castShadow = true;
const footL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.09, 0.06, 0.2), this._mat(0x1c1c1c));
footL.position.set(0, -0.55, 0.04);
legL.add(legLMesh, footL);
legL.position.set(-0.08, 0.7, 0);
const legR = legL.clone(true);
legR.position.x = 0.08;
const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.145, 10, 8), skin);
head.position.y = 1.34;
head.castShadow = true;
const hair = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.152, 10, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI * 0.55), hairMat);
hair.position.y = 1.38;
const armL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.055, 0.3, 3, 6), this._mat(color));
armL.position.set(-0.21, 0.98, 0);
const armR = armL.clone();
armR.position.x = 0.21;
g.add(torso, hips, legL, legR, head, hair, armL, armR);
g.userData = {
arms: [armL, armR], legs: [legL, legR],
phase: this._r() * Math.PI * 2, speed: 0.6 + this._r() * 0.8,
throwTimer: 4 + this._r() * 8, dir: this._r() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1,
side: 1, cleaner: false,
};
return g;
}

_makeVehicle(kind) {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const tyre = this._mat(0x1a1a1a, { roughness: 0.95 });
const addWheel = (x, y, z, r) => {
const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r, r, 0.12, 12), tyre);
w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
w.position.set(x, y, z);
g.add(w);
return w;
};
const addDriver = (x, y, z, color) => {
const d = new THREE.Group();
const torso = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.14, 0.3, 3, 6), this._mat(color));
torso.position.y = 0.24;
const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.13, 8, 7), this._mat(0xc79466));
head.position.y = 0.52;
d.add(torso, head);
d.position.set(x, y, z);
g.add(d);
return d;
};
let body;
if (kind === 'rickshaw') {
body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.95, 0.5, 1.3), this._mat(0xc32b2b, { roughness: 0.6 }));
body.position.set(0, 0.75, -0.35);
const hood = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.5, 0.95, 12, 1, false, 0, Math.PI),
this._mat(0x1f7a4d, { roughness: 0.7 }));
hood.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
hood.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
hood.position.set(0, 1.05, -0.35);
const seat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.82, 0.14, 0.55), this._mat(0x2b2b2b));
seat.position.set(0, 1.02, -0.35);
const seatBack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.82, 0.4, 0.08), this._mat(0xe8b84a, { roughness: 0.6 }));
seatBack.position.set(0, 1.2, -0.6);
const saddle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.09, 0.11, 0.2, 8), this._mat(0x1c1c1c));
saddle.position.set(0, 0.68, 0.55);
const handleBar = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.02, 0.02, 0.5, 6), this._mat(0x2b2b2b));
handleBar.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
handleBar.position.set(0, 0.82, 0.82);
g.add(hood, seat, seatBack, saddle, handleBar);
addDriver(0, 0.42, 0.6, 0xf4a261);
addWheel(-0.36, 0.34, -0.55, 0.34); addWheel(0.36, 0.34, -0.55, 0.34);
addWheel(0, 0.3, 0.82, 0.3);
} else if (kind === 'cng') {
body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 0.85, 1.8), this._mat(0x2a9d8f, { roughness: 0.55 }));
body.position.y = 0.75;
const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.12, 0.12, 1.5), this._mat(0xf2c14e));
roof.position.y = 1.22;
const grille = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.0, 0.5, 0.08), this._mat(0x1c1c1c, { roughness: 0.8 }));
grille.position.set(0, 0.62, -0.9);
g.add(roof, grille);
addDriver(0, 0.5, -0.35, 0x264653);
addWheel(-0.5, 0.3, -0.6, 0.3); addWheel(0.5, 0.3, -0.6, 0.3); addWheel(0, 0.3, 0.75, 0.3);
} else if (kind === 'bus') {
body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.7, 1.6, 4.4), this._mat(0xe9c46a, { roughness: 0.7 }));
body.position.y = 1.15;
const stripe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.74, 0.3, 4.4), this._mat(0xc0392b));
stripe.position.y = 1.0;
const glass = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.72, 0.5, 3.6),
this._mat(0x2c4450, { roughness: 0.25, metalness: 0.4 }));
glass.position.y = 1.62;
g.add(stripe, glass);
addDriver(-0.55, 0.95, 1.85, 0x264653);
addWheel(-0.8, 0.36, -1.4, 0.36); addWheel(0.8, 0.36, -1.4, 0.36);
addWheel(-0.8, 0.36, 1.5, 0.36); addWheel(0.8, 0.36, 1.5, 0.36);
} else {
body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.32, 0.4, 1.3), this._mat(0x37576b, { roughness: 0.5 }));
body.position.y = 0.62;
const rider = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.16, 0.4, 3, 6), this._mat(0xe07a3f));
rider.position.set(0, 1.05, -0.1);
g.add(rider);
addWheel(0, 0.3, -0.55, 0.3); addWheel(0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.3);
}
body.castShadow = true;
g.add(body);
const smoke = new THREE.Group();
for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
const puff = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.1 + i * 0.03, 6, 5),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x555550, transparent: true, opacity: 0 })
);
puff.userData.t = i / 3;
smoke.add(puff);
}
smoke.position.set(0, 0.55, kind === 'bus' ? 2.3 : 0.75);
g.add(smoke);
g.userData = { kind, speed: 1.5 + this._r() * 2.5, dir: this._r() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1, smoke, smokePhase: this._r() * 6 };
return g;
}

_buildTrafficAndPeople() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
const halfW = C.width / 2;
const bankY = C.bankTopY;
const bx = this._bridgeX;
const clothes = [0x2a9d8f, 0xe76f51, 0x264653, 0xf4a261, 0x457b9d, 0xe9c46a, 0x8b5a2b, 0x3d5a80];
// Pedestrians: walk banks, periodically cross via foot-over-bridge, throw litter into drain
const PED_COUNT = 40;
for (let i = 0; i < PED_COUNT; i++) {
const p = this._makePedestrian(clothes[i % clothes.length]);
const near = i % 2 === 0;
const homeZ = near ? (halfW + 1.0 + this._r() * 1.1) : -(halfW + 3.3 - this._r() * 0.7);
const minX = near ? C.westX - 2 : C.westX - 2;
const maxX = near ? C.promenadeEndX - 2 : C.cityEndX - 1.2;
p.position.set(minX + this._r() * (maxX - minX), bankY, homeZ);
p.userData.minX = minX;
p.userData.maxX = maxX;
p.userData.homeZ = homeZ;
p.userData.near = near;
p.userData.side = near ? 1 : -1;
p.userData.baseY = bankY;
p.userData.mode = 'walk';
p.userData.bridgeProgress = 0;
p.userData.bridgeTargetNear = !near;
p.userData.crossTimer = 6 + this._r() * 14;
p.userData.throwTimer = 0.8 + this._r() * 2.5;
p.userData.throwPose = 0;
p.userData.panicTimer = 2 + this._r() * 3.5;
p.userData.cleaner = i === 0;
if (p.userData.cleaner) {
p.position.set(C.binX - 1.5, bankY, C.binZ - 0.8);
p.userData.near = true;
p.userData.homeZ = C.binZ - 0.8;
}
this.group.add(p);
this._pedestrians.push(p);
}
const kindPool = ['rickshaw', 'cng', 'bus', 'bike'];
const VEHICLE_COUNT = 28;
for (let i = 0; i < VEHICLE_COUNT; i++) {
const kind = kindPool[i % kindPool.length];
const v = this._makeVehicle(kind);
const farSide = i % 2 === 0;
const laneZ = farSide
? -(halfW + 5.9 + (i % 4 === 0 ? 0 : 0.85))
: (halfW + 5.85 + (i % 4 === 1 ? 0 : 0.9));
const minX = C.westX - 3;
const maxX = farSide ? (C.cityEndX - 2.5) : (C.promenadeEndX - 3);
const dir = farSide ? (i % 4 === 0 ? 1 : -1) : (i % 4 === 1 ? -1 : 1);
v.userData.dir = dir;
v.userData.minX = minX;
v.userData.maxX = maxX;
v.userData.laneZ = laneZ;
v.userData.bridgeX = bx;
v.userData.kind = kind;
v.userData.phase = this._r() * Math.PI * 2;
v.userData.angryDriver = i % 3 === 1; // denser angry drivers both sides
v.position.set(minX + this._r() * (maxX - minX), bankY, laneZ);
v.rotation.y = dir > 0 ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2;
this.group.add(v);
this._vehicles.push(v);
}
}

_buildAtmosphere() {
const C = LevelConfig.canal;
// clouds drifting over the bay (grow denser from ocean vapor)
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
const c = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.4 + this._r() * 2.6, 8, 5),
new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xd4e4ec, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55, roughness: 1 })
);
c.position.set(-40 + this._r() * 100, 16 + this._r() * 10, -30 - this._r() * 50);
c.scale.set(2.1, 0.5, 1.2);
c.castShadow = false;
this.group.add(c);
this._clouds.push({ mesh: c, kind: 'cloud', speed: 0.15 + this._r() * 0.22, baseScale: 2.1 });
}
// ocean vapor puffs rising into the sky
for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
const p = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.55 + this._r() * 0.5, 6, 5),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xdceaf2, transparent: true, opacity: 0.18 })
);
p.position.set(C.mouthX + 8 + this._r() * 40, 1 + this._r() * 3, -10 - this._r() * 40);
p.castShadow = false;
this.group.add(p);
this._vaporPuffs.push({ mesh: p, phase: this._r() * 6, speed: 0.4 + this._r() * 0.5 });
}
// gulls / kites circling over open water
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
const b = new THREE.Group();
const wing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.9, 0.18),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x2c2c2c, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
const wing2 = wing.clone();
wing.rotation.z = 0.3; wing2.rotation.z = -0.3;
wing.position.x = -0.42; wing2.position.x = 0.42;
b.add(wing, wing2);
this.group.add(b);
this._birds.push({
mesh: b, phase: this._r() * 6, radius: 10 + this._r() * 22, height: 9 + this._r() * 7,
});
}
// haze motes in the sunbeam over the canal
for (let i = 0; i < 28; i++) {
const p = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.04, 4, 4),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfff4dd, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 })
);
p.position.set(C.westX + this._r() * (C.mouthX - C.westX + 14), 1.4 + this._r() * 7, (this._r() - 0.5) * 26);
this.group.add(p);
this._particles.push(p);
}
// rain streaks (activated by weather system)
for (let i = 0; i < 140; i++) {
const drop = new THREE.Mesh(
new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.02, 0.35, 0.02),
new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xb8d0e0, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45 })
);
drop.position.set(
C.westX + this._r() * 55, 4 + this._r() * 12, -20 + this._r() * 40
);
drop.visible = false;
drop.castShadow = false;
this.group.add(drop);
this._rainDrops.push(drop);
}
// trash rafts that accumulate in the bay as pollution rises
for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
const t = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.34, 0.08, 0.28), this._mat(0x8b8577));
t.position.set(C.mouthX + 2 + this._r() * 16, 0.13, -4 - this._r() * 20);
t.rotation.y = this._r() * 3;
t.visible = false;
t.castShadow = false;
this.group.add(t);
this._riverTrash.push(t);
}
// country boats working the bay
for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
const boat = this._makeCountryBoat();
boat.position.set(C.mouthX + 8 + this._r() * 26, 0.2, -8 - this._r() * 26);
boat.rotation.y = this._r() * Math.PI;
this.group.add(boat);
this._boats = this._boats || [];
this._boats.push({ mesh: boat, phase: this._r() * 6, baseY: 0.2 });
}
}

_makeCountryBoat() {
const g = new THREE.Group();
const hull = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.42, 3.4, 4, 8), this._mat(0x6b4a2e, { roughness: 0.9 }));
hull.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
hull.scale.set(1, 1, 0.55);
hull.castShadow = true;
const canopy = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.55, 0.55, 1.6, 10, 1, false, 0, Math.PI),
this._mat(0xb8a37a));
canopy.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
canopy.position.y = 0.35;
const mast = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 2.4, 6), this._mat(0x5a4326));
mast.position.set(-1.1, 1.2, 0);
g.add(hull, canopy, mast);
return g;
}

_finalize() {
this._updateDropVolume();
this._buildEnvironmentMap();
}

// Image-based lighting from the sky gradient: gives metal and water real
// sheen without shipping an HDR file.
_buildEnvironmentMap() {
const renderer = this.sceneManager.renderer;
const tier = this.sceneManager.currentTier;
if (!renderer || !this.skyMesh || !tier || !tier.reflections) return;
if (typeof THREE.PMREMGenerator !== 'function') return;
try {
const pmrem = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
const envScene = new THREE.Scene();
envScene.add(new THREE.Mesh(this.skyMesh.geometry, this.skyMesh.material));
const rt = pmrem.fromScene(envScene, 0, 1, 400);
this.sceneManager.scene.environment = rt.texture;
this._envRT = rt;
pmrem.dispose();
} catch (_) {
// environment lighting is a nicety; ignore failures on weak drivers
}
}

setPollution(p) {
this.pollution = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(p, 0, 100);
const t = this.pollution / 100;
if (this.waterMesh) {
const m = this.waterMesh.material;
m.color.copy(new THREE.Color(0x4b4a34)).lerp(new THREE.Color(0x231c0f), t);
m.roughness = 0.55 + t * 0.35;
m.metalness = 0.16 - t * 0.1;
}
if (this.oceanMesh) {
const m = this.oceanMesh.material;
m.color.copy(new THREE.Color(0x2c7f77)).lerp(new THREE.Color(0x4a4632), t);
m.roughness = 0.3 + t * 0.5;
m.metalness = 0.3 - t * 0.2;
// clean water turns clear enough to see the life underneath
m.opacity = 0.7 + t * 0.29;
}
if (this.skyMesh) {
// Cool sky stays cool; only mild haze when polluted (not yellow sepia)
this.skyMesh.material.color.copy(new THREE.Color(0xffffff)).lerp(new THREE.Color(0xa8b0a4), t * 0.45);
}
this._applyMarineVisibility();
if (this._glitter) this._glitter.visible = t < 0.75;
if (this.sceneManager.scene.fog) {
const fogClean = new THREE.Color(0x8eb8c8);
const fogDirty = new THREE.Color(0x7a8570);
this.sceneManager.scene.fog.color.copy(fogClean).lerp(fogDirty, t * 0.65);
this.sceneManager.scene.background.copy(fogClean).lerp(new THREE.Color(0x6e7a68), t * 0.45);
}
const show = Math.floor(t * this._riverTrash.length);
this._riverTrash.forEach((m, i) => { m.visible = i < show; });
this._birds.forEach((b, i) => { b.mesh.visible = i < Math.ceil((1 - t) * this._birds.length); });
}

setBinHover(on) {
const next = !!on;
if (next && !this._binHovered) this.bus.emit('bin:lid');
this._binHovered = next;
this._lidTarget = next ? -1.5 : 0;
if (this.binGlow) this.binGlow.material.opacity = next ? 0.42 : 0;
if (this.binRing) this.binRing.material.opacity = next ? 0.6 : 0.24;
}

pulseBin() {
if (!this.binGlow) return;
this.binGlow.material.opacity = 0.7;
this._binPulse = 0.45;
}

onPedestrianThrow(cb) { this._throwCallbacks.push(cb); }

signalLitterThrow(position) {
let chosen = null;
let best = Infinity;
for (const p of this._pedestrians) {
if (!p.visible || p.userData.cleaner) continue;
